- **researchzy**: Researchzy, the entry point for research with real papers. Searches about 250 million papers by DOI, arXiv ID, title or keywords, manages the user's library, gets and adds PDFs, and routes every other research task (literature reviews, systematic reviews and meta-analyses, ideas, citations, journal choice, reading, writing, translation and polishing, figures, statistics and causal inference, slides, grants, submission and revision) to the Researchzy skill that does it. Use when the user mentions Researchzy, wants to find, save or get papers, or asks for research help without naming a skill. ([Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills](https://github.com/Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/researchzy/skills/researchzy))
- **researchzy-causal-inference**: Estimate causal effects from panel or cross-section data: probe the design, fix the estimand first, run DiD (staggered, event study), IV, RD, panel FE, synthetic control or weighting with diagnostics, log every specification, check the text. Use for policy evaluations and natural experiments. ([Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills](https://github.com/Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/researchzy-manuscript/skills/researchzy-causal-inference))
- **researchzy-citation-finder**: Find citations for manuscript sentences that need one ([Citation needed: …] placeholders, uncited claims): search Researchzy's index, read each candidate against the sentence, keep a checked quote, write BibTeX. Use to fill the citation gaps of a draft. ([Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills](https://github.com/Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/researchzy/skills/researchzy-citation-finder))
- **researchzy-data-availability**: Draft or audit manuscript Data/Code Availability statements, dataset access routes, repository plans, and FAIR metadata. Use for Data Availability statements, data sharing, repository choice and dataset citations; not general data cleaning or statistical analysis. ([Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills](https://github.com/Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/researchzy-manuscript/skills/researchzy-data-availability))
- **researchzy-deep-read**: Close reading of one paper as a Markdown long-read: how the authors actually got there, which design choices carry the result, the key formulas worked through with concrete numbers, and how far the evidence reaches, including what the paper does not report. Use to understand a paper before building on, presenting, reviewing or citing it. Not a summary, a translation or a peer review. ([Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills](https://github.com/Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/researchzy-reading/skills/researchzy-deep-read))
- **researchzy-download**: Download the lawful full text of identified papers by DOI, exact title or a known PDF URL. Tries the PDF Researchzy holds, then open-access copies (PMC, Unpaywall, openly licensed publisher PDFs, arXiv) and Elsevier, Springer Nature or IEEE full text through the user's own API keys, and records every attempt in a per-paper manifest. Use when the user wants papers' PDFs or full text, or another skill needs a paper Researchzy has no PDF for. ([Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills](https://github.com/Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/researchzy-reading/skills/researchzy-download))
- **researchzy-figures**: Make publication figures from data: profile the data, choose a chart it can support, draw it with matplotlib at the venue's column width, export PDF and PNG with a record, and check the file's size, fonts and colours. Use for a new figure, or to check a figure file before submission. ([Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills](https://github.com/Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/researchzy-manuscript/skills/researchzy-figures))
- **researchzy-find-journal**: Shortlist journals or conferences for a manuscript from evidence: where similar recent papers and its references appeared, recent on-topic papers per venue, Crossref and DOAJ records. ([Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills](https://github.com/Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/researchzy-manuscript/skills/researchzy-grants))
- **researchzy-ideas**: Develop research ideas: generate them from a topic, an observation or a review's gaps, sharpen one hypothesis, or evaluate an idea you already have. ([Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills](https://github.com/Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/researchzy/skills/researchzy-ideas))
- **researchzy-latex**: Build a LaTeX paper with its own engine and bibliography tool, turn the log into a short problem list, fix only mechanical problems with a content check, move a paper into a venue kit the user supplies, and pack a source archive that rebuilds. Use when a paper will not compile or before submission. ([Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills](https://github.com/Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/researchzy-manuscript/skills/researchzy-latex))
- **researchzy-literature-review**: Write a literature review from real papers: scope the questions, search Researchzy's index with a logged strategy, screen, read the core papers in full, and synthesize by theme with a comparison table, gaps and BibTeX.
